## 18. Acceptance

Each item is a test or a command whose output decides it. "Private server": a tmux server on a socket in a test-owned `0700` directory, killed at test end. "Stub": the stub agent `crates/zygo-launch/tests/stub_agent/stub.rs` (binary `stub-agent`, `crates/zygo-launch/Cargo.toml:39-40`), extended to: record `--session-id`/`--resume`/`-s`; call the `SessionStart` hook with that id; emit `UserPromptSubmit`, `PreToolUse` (`Agent`), `Stop`, `StopFailure`, `SubagentStart` and `SubagentStop` hook calls when a control file asks; make a model request through its proxy (held open for a given time) when asked; stay alive until a signal; exit with a code from the control file.

**Identity, state and validation**
1. Two linked worktrees and the main checkout of one repository give the same id; another repository gives another id. Two repositories named `app` get names `app` and `app-2`.
2. Slug: `My Repo!!` gives `my-repo`; `---` gives `ws`; a 50-character name gives 32 characters with no trailing `-`; the collision suffix keeps the name at 32 characters or less.
3. A bare repository `repo.git` with one worktree `main`: `up` names the workspace after the worktree and opens panes in it; with no worktree, `up` exits 2 (AU77).
4. `workspace.json` is mode `0600` in a `0700` directory and holds no value of the session token file, no provider key, no `zyg_` string, and none of these stub arguments: a positional prompt, `--append-system-prompt <text>`, `--resume <x>`. It keeps `--append-system-prompt-file <path>` with the path only, as an absolute path.
5. A member launched with `--agents '{"r":{"description":"d","prompt":"p"}}'`, `--mcp-config <existing file>` and `--plugin-url https://h/p.zip`: `workspace.json` holds `--mcp-config <path>`, no JSON, no URL, and `dropped_flags = ["--agents", "--plugin-url"]`; the next `up` prints one warning per dropped flag.
6. A member launched with `-- --resume abc "fix it"` and one with `-- -r --model lead`: the saved `args` are empty and `--model lead` (AU30).
7. A write killed between the temporary file and the rename leaves the old `workspace.json` readable and unchanged; a `.tmp*` file older than 1 min is gone after the next write.
8. Two writers at once: both changes are in the file; a snapshot that cannot get the lock in 1 s sets `snapshot.pending`, and the next writer runs the snapshot.
9. Two snapshots started at once after one new split, and a hand-started member keying the same pane: the pane has exactly one `@zygo-pane` key, and `panes[]` holds it once (AU41, AU79).
10. A pane whose `@zygo-pane` is `k$(x)` or `#{pane_id}`, a session whose `@zygo-ws` is `abc`, and a `workspace.json` with `name = "a b"` or `tmux_session = "other"`: the snapshot writes one `errors.log` line and changes nothing; `up` and `down` exit 1 (AU84).
11. `version = 2` in `workspace.json`: `up` exits 1 with the newer-zygo text and does not change the file. A version 1 file with an extra field keeps it after a write (AU78).
12. A pane cwd that holds the field separator and a `:` is read whole, and a `list-panes` line with a missing field is refused with one `errors.log` line (AU99).

**up and restore (private server, stub)**
13. First `up --no-attach` with loadout `pair` creates `zygo-<name>` with the loadout's windows, panes and layout; the session has `ZYGO_WORKSPACE`, `destroy-unattached off` and `remain-on-exit off` while the global values are `on`; no global option or global hook changed (`show-options -g`, `show-hooks -g` equal before and after); no `after-select-pane` or `after-select-window` hook is set; a `window-unlinked` hook is set (on each window on tmux 3.2, on the session on tmux 3.3 and later; PW6 6.30).
14. `workspace.json` holds a `panes[]` entry for every pane before the first `ws run` reads it; `tmux_socket` is an absolute path equal to `display-message -p '#{socket_path}'`; every pane's start command is `ws run`, with `--shell` for shell panes; a pane split by hand later opens in the main worktree.
15. Each hook command holds the literal id and the quoted zygo path and no `#{@` reference. A zygo path that holds `'` or `$` makes `up` exit 2 before it writes `workspace.json` or starts tmux. A socket path that holds `$` (through `TMUX_TMPDIR`) makes `up` kill the new session, set `phase = down` and exit 2 (AU54).
16. A worktree directory named `#(touch pwned)` as a pane cwd, a main worktree path with `#` in it, and a window named `#(touch pwned)` by hand: after `down` and `up`, no file `pwned` exists, the pane is in that directory, and the window name is the same text (AU55). A window with `automatic-rename` on has no saved name.
17. `up` run with `ZYGO_SESSION`, `ZYGO_SESSION_DIR`, `ZYGO_AGENT`, `ZYGO_URL`, `ZYGO_REPO`, `ZYGO_WORK`, `ZYGO_MAIN_ROLE`, `CLAUDECODE=1`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID=x`, `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:1/anthropic`, `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_HEADERS=X-Zygo-Token: zyg_t_x` and `ZYGO_TEST_KEY=k` set: no pane has any of the first eleven; the panes have `ZYGO_TEST_KEY`; a catalog provider with `key = "env:ZYGO_TEST_KEY"` serves a request from a pane's agent (mock upstream receives `k`); the agent child of each launcher has no `ZYGO_TEST_KEY` (AU64). The same `up` does not attach (AU95).
18. After an agent exits in a `ws run` pane, the pane's shell has `ZYGO_WORKSPACE=<id>` and no `ZYGO_PANE`; `zygo claude` started in that shell joins as a member (AU93).
19. A loadout pane with `compact = "600k"`, `config_dir = "<dir>"` and `server = "https://127.0.0.1:<port>"` gives `LaunchOpts.compact` 600000, `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR = <dir>` in the child environment, and `LaunchOpts.server` that URL. After `down` and `up` with no change in the session, the resume gets the same `--compact 600k` (AU32). A loadout pane with `server = "https://u:p@h"` is reported by `zygo config check`, and a hand launch with it writes no member record (AU98).
20. The stub in a Claude Code pane received `--session-id=<uuid>` (one token, AU3); `workspace.json` holds that id. After `down` then `up`, the stub receives `--resume=<same id>` and no other session argument; each shell pane is a shell in its saved cwd; the window layout string and the window size equal the saved ones; the saved active pane is active.
21. A shell pane that ran `sleep 1000` at `down` comes back as a shell, and no `sleep` process runs after `up`.
22. `kill-server` on the private server, then `up`: every member resumes with its id; the layout equals the last written one; the first `SessionStart` output contains the "did not stop this session cleanly" note. `up --all` after `kill-server` restores a workspace that had `phase = up` (AU52).
23. `kill-session` by hand: 5 s later `windows[]`, `panes[]`, `snapshot_seq` and `snapshot_at` of `workspace.json` are unchanged and each agent member is `resumable` (its member wrapper's exit write, AU17); then `up`: every member resumes and the layout equals the layout before the kill (AU89).
24. With a second tmux session created after the workspace session and no client (so tmux does not take the workspace as current): `kill-pane` on one agent pane: within 5 s its key is gone from `panes[]` and its member record stays; `up` after `down` does not recreate it and prints the detached line; `zygo ws forget-pane <key>` deletes the record and its results cache. `kill-window` on a window with an agent pane gives the same result. `kill-pane` on the shell pane that is not the active pane of a window of two shell panes: within 5 s its key is gone (W§7.4, W§7.5).
25. The stub exits 0 by itself: the pane becomes a shell; after `down`, `up` it is a shell. The stub exits 130 by itself: `ended`, and the resume command is printed. A stub killed by SIGINT after a SIGTERM to the launcher: `resumable` (AU73).
26. A resume where the stub exits 3 at once: the pane is a shell, the output names the id and the resume command, `member.state` stays `resumable`; the next `up` tries the resume again (AU60).
27. A resume whose launch exits 13 (before the callback) and one that exits 12 (after the callback, before the spawn): `member.zygo_session`, `agent_session` and `state` are unchanged and `member.pending` is none after each; the next `up` resumes it. A member that reached its first `SessionStart` has `pending` committed and `clean_stop`, `closed_units` cleared (AU60, AU92).
28. A member whose cwd worktree was deleted: the pane opens a shell in the main worktree, prints the AU61 resume command, and does not resume. A member launched with `--config-dir <dir>`: the resume runs with that `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`; with the agent moved to another `PATH` directory, the resume uses the one on `PATH` (AU61).
29. `up --reset` on a stopped workspace lists the dropped agent session ids and applies the loadout; `up --reset` on a running workspace exits 1; `up` in a directory that is not a repository exits 2.
30. A result stored before `down` is readable with `result_view` by the same id after `up`; the new session's `results` is a symlink to the workspace results cache; after the launcher exits and a sweep runs 24 h or more later, the old session directory is gone and the result file stays (AU16, AU69).
31. `zygo claude` started by hand in a new split of a workspace session with no session argument: the stub receives `--session-id=<uuid>` (one token, AU3); it gets a pane key and a member record, and resumes after `down`/`up` (AU9).
32. `zygo claude` started by hand on a pane key whose member is `resumable` with an id: exit 1 with the AU94 text and no change to the record; on a key whose member is `ended`: it joins.
33. A resumed member with each bypass form (`--dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--allow-dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`, `yolo = true`): `up` and `up --all` print it and resume it (AU70).
34. With `TMUX_TMPDIR=<dir A>`, `up` creates the workspace; with `TMUX_TMPDIR=<dir B>` and `$TMUX` naming another server, `ls`, `down` and `up` reach it through `tmux_socket`. With the socket file removed while the stub launchers run, `up` exits 1 with the "has live agents" text and starts no agent; `down` exits 1 too (AU62, AU87). A saved socket whose parent directory has mode `0755` makes `up` exit 1 naming the path. A saved socket whose parent directory exists and is owned by root (`/`) makes `up` exit 1 with the same line: the owner test of that check (`crates/zygo-workspace/src/up.rs:308`) refuses it; the case is skipped, with a printed line, when the test runs as root or `/` is not owned by root. A socket file of another owner is refused by the same check (`up.rs:311-312`); only that part needs root to set up, so code reading verifies it.
35. A tmux session named `zygo-<name>` whose `@zygo-ws` is another id: `up`, `attach` and `down` exit 1 with the AU63 text and do not touch it.
36. A stub `tmux` that fails `has-session` with `permission denied`: `up` and `down` exit 1 and change nothing; the snapshot writes one `errors.log` line (AU87).
37. Kill `up` with SIGKILL after it created the first window of two: the next `up` creates the missing panes, and `phase` becomes `up` (AU67).
38. A saved window of 80×24 with 8 panes in `even-vertical`, restored with `up --no-attach`: every pane is built or reported not built; each not-built pane is gone from `panes[]` and its member stays `resumable`; `phase = up` (AU96).
39. `up` inside a tmux client of another server prints the AU100 attach command and does not run `switch-client`.

**down and drain**
40. A stub member that is idle: `down` ends within 5 s; `phase = down`; no tmux session.
41. A stub member in a turn (after `UserPromptSubmit`, before `Stop`): `down` waits; after `Stop` and 10 s without a model request it stops. The same with `StopFailure` (AU57).
42. A stub member with `busy = false` and a model request held open for 20 s: `down` waits until 10 s after the request ends (AU26, AU91). With a stub team server that has no `requests_in_flight` field, `down` prints the "rule skipped" line and stops after `Stop`.
43. A stub member with `SubagentStart` and no `SubagentStop`, and a `Stop` with an empty `background_tasks`: `down` waits until `SubagentStop` (AU85). Events with an `agent_id` key leave `busy` unchanged (AU43).
44. During the drain the `pre-tool` hook on an `Agent` input prints the deny JSON with the exact reason; with `drain_block_dispatch = false` it prints nothing. Kill `down` with SIGKILL during the drain: the hook prints nothing at once (AU83). In phase `up` the hook does not open `op.lock` (strace or a lock probe).
45. During `stopping`, a `UserPromptSubmit` input gets the block JSON with `zygo: workspace is stopping`, and an `Agent` input is denied with the same reason; `down` survives SIGINT, SIGHUP and SIGTERM sent to it in that phase (AU86).
46. A hook holds `op.lock` for 100 ms while `up` starts: `up` succeeds (AU83).
47. `down --timeout 2s` with a busy member exits 1, lists the member and `--now`, sets `phase = up`, and frees `op.lock`.
48. `down --now` with a running subagent: the member stops at once; after `up` the first `SessionStart` output contains the interrupted-subagent note with its id and type, and the second does not.
49. A member with an open work unit and one with a unit ended by `SubagentStop`: after `up` the closed-units note names only the first; the usage store has both rows with end times (AU31, AU76).
50. `down` with `ZYGO_SESSION` set returns within 1 s with `zygo: down started; end your turn`; the detached process completes the drain with the same `--timeout`; `ls` shows `draining` while it runs. `down --all` with `ZYGO_SESSION` set writes to `down-all.log`, and a log over 64 KiB is cut before the start (AU97).
51. A detached `down` killed with SIGKILL in `draining`: `up` sets `phase = up`. Killed in `stopping` after all launchers ended: the next `up` or `down` kills the session and sets `phase = down`. Killed in `stopping` with a launcher still live: `phase = up`, and that member has `clean_stop = false` (AU75). A `down` killed after `kill-session` and before the `phase` write: the next `up` sets `phase = down` and restores.
52. A member whose launcher died by SIGKILL, with its `pid` file naming a live unrelated process: `down` sends that process no signal and reads no status for it (AU74).
53. `down --all` with two good workspaces, one whose `op.lock` is held, one with a corrupt `workspace.json`, and one with `phase = up` and no session: the good ones stop with `down_all = true`, the locked and corrupt ones are reported, the last gets `phase = down` and `down_all = true`, and the exit code is 1. With one idle and one busy workspace, the idle one reaches `phase = down` while the busy one still drains (AU19). `down --all` where one workspace times out: `down_all` is set only on the others.

**Membership and isolation**
54. `zygo claude` (stub) outside tmux, and inside a tmux session that is not a workspace, with `ZYGO_WORKSPACE` and `ZYGO_PANE` in its environment: the child environment has neither; no workspace hooks in the generated settings; no state written (OP9, AU58).
55. A `zygo claude` started from inside a member's tool (`ZYGO_SESSION` set) does not join.
56. `zygo claude -- --settings <file>` in a workspace pane, once with a readable file and once with a missing file: the launch is a member (its member record is written, the generated settings hold the workspace hooks), the agent gets only zygo's `--settings` file, stderr has the main spec §8.5 Flags line (merge, or the value not used) and no workspace line, and the pane resumes after `down`/`up` (AU59).
57. Two hook processes of one member write `activity.json` at the same time 100 times: every change is in the file (AU56).
58. `ws run` whose launcher receives SIGHUP and SIGTERM while the agent runs forwards them, still writes the member record after the agent ends, and the shell after `exec` has default SIGHUP and SIGTERM handling (AU88).
59. `zygo ws snapshot` does not sweep: an ended session directory older than 24 h is still there after 10 snapshots, and is gone after one `zygo status` (AU71).
60. A resumed member's note inputs are in the session view after the launcher saved the view, and the first `SessionStart` prints them (AU72).
61. The results sweep never deletes a workspace results folder: with a `workspace.json` that does not parse, and for a pane key that has no member record, every result stays (AU69).

**Config and platform**
62. `[loadout.x]` in `.zygo.toml` on the default branch is ignored with the existing warning; in the team layer it is dropped with the AU22 warning.
63. `zygo config check` reports a pane with `kind = "vim"` and `passthrough = true` on an OpenCode pane, and does not report a pane with `args = ["--settings", "x"]` (AU59).
64. On Windows (CI job), `zygo up`, `down`, `ls`, `attach` exit 2 with `workspaces need tmux; run zygo in WSL`; `zygo claude` with the stub passes the v1 launch test.
65. With a stub `tmux` that prints `tmux 3.1`, `up` exits 2 naming the version.

**Slash commands, saved overrides, OpenCode, membership path**
66. In a member, `/zygo:down` returns within the hook bound with `zygo: down started; end your turn`, the prompt does not reach the model and does not set `busy`, and the detached `down` stops the workspace. `/zygo:down --now` stops at once. `/zygo:ls` lists the workspaces; `/zygo:workspace` prints this workspace's members. `/zygo:up` and `/zygo:attach` block with the `/zygo:help` text, as every name that is not a verb (OP11; base spec §20 #17c). `/zygo:help` lists `down` with the arguments of `zygo down` and a summary that says `/zygo:down` takes only `--now` (`crates/zygo-core/src/verbs.rs:34-39`).
67. In a member, `zygo results off` (and `/zygo:guards <guard> <level>`): `member.overrides` equals the new `overrides.json` at once. Then `kill-server` and `up`: the new session's `overrides.json` has the same content before the first hook call, with `clean_stop = false` (OP12, AU105).
68. In a member, `/zygo:redaction content off`: after `down` and `up`, the proxy session's settings (`PATCH` reply or status) show `redaction_content = false` before the agent's first request (W§8.2 step 4).
69. In a member launched with `--compact 300k`: `zygo compact 600k` gives `member.compact = set 600000`, and the resume gets `--compact 600000`; `zygo compact off` gives `set off`, and the resume gets `--compact off`; `zygo compact on` gives `cleared`, and the resume gets no `--compact`; with no change, the resume gets `--compact 300k` (AU32). A call of the `compact` MCP tool leaves `member.compact` unchanged.
70. With `workspace.json` locked by a test for 2 s, `zygo results off` in a member replies with `; not saved for the workspace` and exits as without it (AU105).
71. A loadout pane with `args = ["--settings", "<file>"]`: the pane runs as a member with the member hooks; its member record is written as for any member, and `member.launch.args` keep `--settings <file>` (AU30, AU59, AU102).
72. `ws run` with a stub `tmux` that fails every call still launches the member with the right `LaunchOpts.member` (it does no tmux detection) (AU102).
73. The callback write: after one start, `member.launch`, `config_dir`, `dropped_flags` and `program` are written with `pending` in one write; moving the agent to another `PATH` directory and restarting refreshes `program` (AU92).
74. OpenCode stub member: the plugin's load-time `session` call commits `pending`, and its first `sessionID` event records `agent_session`; on resume the note text appears in the system transform output (AU104). The check runs the generated plugin file under `node` as a proxy for the plugin logic; OpenCode runs plugins in Bun, and the live W§17 PW4 check covers that runtime.
75. `cargo tree -p zygo-workspace` shows no `zygo-launch` and no `zygo-hooks`; the workspace builds with no dependency cycle (AU103).

**Live checks (manual, recorded)**
76. PW1, PW2, PW3, PW5, PW7, PW9, PW10, PW15 (for the Bash tool, the `workspace_down` tool and `/zygo:down`, each with and without the sandbox, AU65), PW16 on the pinned Claude Code; PW4 on the pinned OpenCode; PW6 and PW11 on tmux 3.2.

**Added after the P1 live checks (2026-11-22)**
77. The setup of #24. Each case has its own version boundary; both are 3.8, the lowest probed version that runs the workspace hook while another session is current (window: `fixtures/tmux/e1-hooks-<version>.txt` case D; active pane: `fixtures/tmux/e1-active-pane-<version>.txt`, `kill-pane on pane 0`; W§7.4). On tmux before 3.8 (the version from `tmux -V`): `kill-window` on a window of shell panes only: `panes[]` still holds their keys 5 s later; then `kill-pane` on the active pane of another window of two shell panes, and a `split-window` in a third window: the keys of the closed window and of the closed pane are gone within 5 s (W§7.4 known limit). On tmux 3.8 and later: after the `kill-window`, the keys of the window are gone within 5 s; after the `kill-pane`, its key is gone within 5 s; there is no other trigger (W§7.4).
78. With `[features] workspace_tool = true`, the zygo MCP server of a member lists `workspace_down`. A call runs `zygo down -- <name>` with `ZYGO_SESSION` set and returns `zygo: down started; end your turn`; `now = true` runs `zygo down --now -- <name>`. A name that starts with `-` (AU84) is passed after `--`, not refused. With the toggle off, or in a session that is not a member, the tool is not listed. A `zygo down` that does not return in 5 s is killed, and the call is a tool error (AU65).
79. On tmux 3.2a and on the tmux first on `PATH`: `up --no-attach` of a saved first window of 173×47 with 3 panes gives a first window of 173×47 with the saved pane sizes. After `up`, `#{window-size}` of that window is the global value, not `manual`. A client of 100×30 that attaches later gives 100×29 (AU96).
80. `down` with `ZYGO_SESSION` set and stdin, stdout and stderr as pipes: the parent exits without waiting for the drain or for the detached process, and the pipes reach EOF when the parent exits (the test deadline is 5 s; the detached process lives longer, so EOF before the deadline proves it holds no pipe). With an injected mount namespace that differs from the tmux server's, or cannot be read, `down` exits 1 with the AU18 sandbox text and starts nothing (AU18).

**Added after the P2 live checks (2026-11-22)**
81. A member whose `activity.json` has `busy = true` and an empty running set, and whose proxy reports no model request in flight and none that ended in the last 10 s: `down` never counts it as idle and prints no interrupted-turn line; at the drain timeout it writes `phase = up`, stops nothing, lists the member with `no Stop since its last prompt` and exits 1. A `Stop` during the drain lets `down` go on and stop the workspace. A member with `busy = true` and a request in flight is busy too (AU26, PW7 7.2).
82. A stub member sends `UserPromptSubmit` and then no `Stop` and no `StopFailure` (a turn ended by Esc, PW7 7.2), then a `SubagentStop` with an empty `agent_type` and no matching `SubagentStart` (PW7 7.5): the running set in `activity.json` stays empty; `down` does not stop the member: at its drain timeout it exits 1 with `phase = up` and the stub still running; `down --now` then stops the workspace with `phase = down` (AU26).
83. `drain::idle` (AU26): a request in flight is busy whatever `last_request_end` holds, also a text that is not RFC 3339; a proxy status that cannot be read is busy with `proxy status not read: <error>`; an `activity.json` that cannot be read or parsed is busy with `activity file unreadable: <error>`, and a missing one is idle; rule 3 is skipped when the status has no `requests_in_flight`, or when no request is in flight and `last_request_end` is not RFC 3339 or is more than 10 s after the poll time; a `last_request_end` 10 s or less after the poll time is busy.
84. A member with `busy = true`, an empty running set, and a proxy status with no `requests_in_flight` field (an older team server): `down` prints `zygo: <member>: proxy request state not usable; idle rule 3 skipped` once, and at the drain timeout exits 1 and lists the member with `no Stop since its last prompt`. A member whose proxy status cannot be read is listed at the timeout with `proxy status not read: <error>` (AU26, W§9.3 step 6).
85. `activity.json` writes (AU56): a hook write to a file that does not parse or is larger than 1 MiB writes nothing and the file stays busy for the drain; a result larger than 1 MiB is not written; a read error other than a missing file writes nothing; a file with a `version` above 1 is not changed and the hook logs `<path>: written by a newer zygo (version <n>)`; `down` reads the known fields of such a file.

**Added with the L2 plan (2026-11-22)**
86. OpenCode stub member, hook level: `zygo hook workspace --agent opencode --event prompt` with `{"sessionID": <id>, "cwd": <directory>}` sets `busy = true` and `tools_running = 0` in `activity.json`; `--event stop` sets `busy = false` and `tools_running = 0`; `--event session` with `{"cwd": <directory>}` commits the pending start of its zygo session, with `{"sessionID": <id>, "cwd": <directory>}` also writes `member.agent_session`, and neither changes `activity.json`, also when `busy` is `true` (AU27, AU104).

**Added with the H1 re-plan (2026-11-22)**
87. Clear rule (AU60): a member record with `pending` of zygo session S and saved values with `saved_for` = S0: a commit of S (the Claude Code first `SessionStart`, an OpenCode `session` call) clears them, `saved_for` and `note_pending`; with `saved_for` = S (a `down --now` during S's first turn wrote them before the commit) it keeps `closed_units`, `interrupted`, `clean_stop` and `saved_for` and clears `note_pending`. `down` writes `saved_for` = the live session's ULID with the saved values (W§9.3 step 8); the AU75 repair removes it.
88. Note rule (W§9.4, OpenCode): inputs with no `agent_session`, then `session` A: the inputs get A and `session-start` prints the note; then `session` B: the inputs are removed and `session-start` prints no note. Inputs with `agent_session` X: `session` X keeps them, `session` Y removes them. A `session` call with no id never removes them.
89. A launch that is not a member: `zygo hook prompt` reads no session view, and `session-start` reads it as before H1; with `view.json` a link, empty, not JSON or unreadable, both give the output or error line of the code before H1; with `view.json` a FIFO, `session-start` gives the `SessionView::load` error `<view.json path>: is a FIFO` with no wait (FIX-22, W§9.1), and `zygo hook prompt` reads no view; neither writes `session.log`, `activity.json` or a state folder (W-C6).

**Added with H3 (2026-11-22)**
90. Plugin event orders (AU27), under node with a stub zygo that records each `zygo hook workspace` call, from plugin load L; `created(X)` and `created(X<P)` are `session.created` with `info.id` X (and `parentID` P), `updated(X)` is `session.updated` with `info.id` X, `deleted(X)` is `session.deleted` with `info.id` X, `busy(X)` a `session.status` `busy`, `idle(X)` a `session.idle`; `session{}` is a `session` call with no `sessionID`: (1) L, created(A), busy(A), idle(A) → `session{}`, `session A`, `prompt A`, `stop A`; (2) L, busy(A), idle(A) → `session{}`, `prompt A`, `stop A`; (3) L, busy(A), updated(A), idle(A) → `session{}`, `prompt A`, `session A`, `stop A`; (4) L, created(A), busy(A), created(K<A), busy(K), idle(K), idle(A), (5) L, created(A), busy(A), busy(K), created(K<A), idle(K), idle(A), (6) L, created(A), busy(A), busy(K1), busy(K2), created(K1<A), created(K2<A), idle(A), and (7) L, created(A), busy(A), busy(K), idle(K), created(K<A), idle(A) → each the calls of (1); (8) L, busy(K), created(K<P) → `session{}`, `prompt K`, `stop K`; (9) the events of (1), then busy(B), updated(B), idle(B) → the calls of (1), then `prompt B`, `session B`, `stop B`; (10) the events of (1), then busy(B), idle(B) → the calls of (1), then `prompt B`, `stop B`; (11) L, created(A), busy(A), created(B), busy(B), idle(A), idle(B) → `session{}`, `session A`, `prompt A`, `session B`, `stop B`; (12) L, busy(Y), created(X), busy(X), updated(Y) → `session{}`, `prompt Y`, `session X`; (13) the events of (1), then created(B), deleted(A) → the calls of (1), then `session B`; (14) without `ZYGO_WORKSPACE`, the events of (1) → no call; (15) the events of (1) at once, with a stub that answers `session` after 0.3 s → the calls of (1) in that order; (16) the events of (1), then created(B), busy(B), idle(B), deleted(B) → the calls of (1), then `session B`, `prompt B`, `stop B`, `session A` (a deleted main session moves the record to the newest known top session); (17) the events of (1), then busy(Y), idle(Y), deleted(Y) → the calls of (1), then `prompt Y`, `stop Y` (a deleted session is never sent); (18) L, a `session.created` of K whose `info.parentID` is `""` or `0`, then busy(K), idle(K) → `session{}` (K is a child); L, a `session.created` of K whose `info.parentID` is `null` → `session{}`, `session K` (K is a top session).
91. Plugin tool orders (AU27 tool count, W§9.1), the harness and notation of #90; `before(T,X,c)` and `after(T,X,c)` are the tool hooks of tool T in session X with `callID` c; a `pre-tool` is written with its `tool_name`; every member list ends with `pre-tool A Agent`, the closing `task` call: (1) L, created(A), busy(A), before(bash,A,a1), after(bash,A,a1), idle(A) → `session{}`, `session A`, `prompt A`, `pre-tool A bash`, `tool-end A`, `stop A`; (2) L, busy(X), before(bash,X,x1), after(bash,X,x1), idle(X) → `session{}`, `prompt X`, `pre-tool X bash`, `tool-end X`, `stop X`; (3) L, created(A), created(K<A), before(bash,K,k1), after(bash,K,k1) → `session{}`, `session A`; (4) L, before(bash,K,k1), created(K<A), after(bash,K,k1) → `session{}`, `pre-tool K bash`, `tool-end K`; (5) L, after(bash,A,z9) → `session{}`; L, before(bash,A,""), after(bash,A,"") → `session{}`, `pre-tool A bash`; L, before(bash,A,a1), after(bash,A,a1), after(bash,A,a1) → `session{}`, `pre-tool A bash`, `tool-end A`; (6) L, before(task,A,t1), after(task,A,t1), created(A), created(K<A), before(task,K,t2), after(task,K,t2) → `session{}`, `pre-tool A Agent`, `tool-end A`, `session A`, `pre-tool K Agent`, `tool-end K`; with a stub whose `pre-tool` denies, L, before(task,A,t1), after(task,A,t1), before(bash,A,b1), after(bash,A,b1) → the `task` hook throws, then `session{}`, `pre-tool A Agent`, `pre-tool A bash`, `tool-end A`; (7) L, created(A), busy(A), before(bash,A,a1), idle(A), busy(A) → `session{}`, `session A`, `prompt A`, `pre-tool A bash`, `stop A`, `prompt A`; (8) without `ZYGO_WORKSPACE`, the steps of (1) → no workspace call, and the guard, trim, work and guard calls; (9) the steps of (1) with a zygo that fails every call, and with one whose `pre-tool` hangs past the 10 s kill → the calls of (1) in that order; with the hang, the steps return in less than 2 s; (10) L, before(bash,A,a1), before(read,A,a2), after(bash,A,a1), after(read,A,a2), and the same with the two `after` in the other order → each `session{}`, `pre-tool A bash`, `pre-tool A read`, `tool-end A`, `tool-end A`; (11) L, busy(A), before(bash,A,a1), idle(A), busy(A), before(bash,A,a2), after(bash,A,a1), after(bash,A,a2) → `session{}`, `prompt A`, `pre-tool A bash`, `stop A`, `prompt A`, `pre-tool A bash`, `tool-end A`; L, before(bash,A,a1), busy(A), after(bash,A,a1) → `session{}`, `pre-tool A bash`, `prompt A`; L, busy(A), before(bash,K,k1), idle(A), busy(A), before(bash,A,a2), after(bash,K,k1), after(bash,A,a2) → `session{}`, `prompt A`, `pre-tool K bash`, `stop A`, `prompt A`, `pre-tool A bash`, `tool-end A` (a `stop` forgets the counted calls of every session: no `tool-end K`); (12) L, before(bash,A,c1), before(bash,B,c1), after(bash,B,c1), after(bash,A,c1) → `session{}`, `pre-tool A bash`, `pre-tool B bash`, `tool-end B`, `tool-end A`.

## 20. Brief corrections

The brief had these facts. The repository says otherwise; this document follows the repository.

1. The brief said that no `Stop` or `SessionEnd` hook is registered today. Correct for `Stop`, `SessionEnd`, `SubagentStart`, `StopFailure` and `Notification`. But `SubagentStop` is registered, only when work units are on (`crates/zygo-launch/src/claude_code.rs:248-254`).
2. The brief said to follow the XDG conventions of `paths.rs`. `paths.rs` has no XDG handling: it uses `$HOME/.config`, `$HOME/.cache` and `$HOME/.local/share` and reads no `XDG_*` variable (`crates/zygo-core/src/paths.rs:27-35`). The state directory uses `$HOME/.local/state` by the same rule.
3. The brief put the compaction override in `overrides.json`. `overrides.json` holds `results`, `results_threshold`, `redaction_content`, `guards` and `work_units` only (`crates/zygo-core/src/overrides.rs:10-19`). The compaction override is proxy-side session state (§7.2, §10.2.4). Both are saved on change for members (OP12, AU105).
4. The brief said "the subagent tool". On the pinned Claude Code the tool is `Agent` (`fixtures/claude-code/hooks/PreToolUse-Agent-main.json`), not `Task`.
5. The brief put the allowed tables of the repository file at `repo_file.rs` about 38 and 197. The allowed list is at `:38`; the filter is at `:218-228`; `:197` is `read_default_branch`.
6. Launcher line ranges: `Launcher::run` with `Launcher::launch` is `launcher.rs:195-442`, `Session::run` is `:447-665`, `finish` is `:729-754`.
7. CLI: `Cmd` is at `crates/zygo/src/cmd/mod.rs:131-241`; dispatch is at `:394-418`. `Launch` has a `--config-dir` flag (`:103-105`) that the brief did not list; the member record keeps it.
8. Not in the brief, found in the repo: the `Stop` input has `background_tasks` (`fixtures/claude-code/hooks/Stop-main.json`); zygo does not read it (AU85). `SessionStart` also fires inside subagents with an `agent_id` key (`SessionStart-sub.json`), so capture skips those (the existing hook already skips them, `crates/zygo-hooks/src/session_start.rs:19-21`).
9. Not in the brief, found in the repo: result ids are read only from the current session's directory (`crates/zygo-mcp/src/tools/results.rs:9-10`) and swept after 24 h (`crates/zygo-results/src/sweep.rs:6`), so a resumed conversation would lose its result ids without AU16.
10. Not in the brief, found in the repo: the launcher folds and ends open work units at exit (`launcher.rs:731`), so units are closed, not open, after `down` (AU31).
11. Not in the brief, found in the repo: the status reply's `compact` is `Option<WindowArg>` (`crates/zygo-proxy/src/session.rs:88-91`, `:273`). AU32 therefore keeps its own three-state value, written on the change path (AU105).
12. Critic review evidence: the launcher denylist is at `crates/zygo-launch/src/env.rs:21-23` (`PREFIXES` at `:21`, `EXACT` at `:22-23`). `claude_code.rs:20` (`SETTINGS_WARN`), `launcher.rs:458` (exit 12 before the spawn at `:639`) and `crates/zygo/src/cmd/mod.rs:548-551` (the sweep for every verb except `hook` and `mcp`) hold. `StopFailure` is in the pinned settings schema (`fixtures/claude-code/settings-schema.json:2228`).
13. Critic read 2 evidence: the proxy reads `env:` keys from its own process environment (`SystemKeys`, `crates/zygo-proxy/src/provider.rs:19-25`), so AU64 keeps provider keys. The launcher sets `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`, `ANTHROPIC_CUSTOM_HEADERS` (with `X-Zygo-Token` in passthrough), the model aliases, `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`, `CLAUDE_CODE_GATEWAY_HINT_HEADERS` and `DISABLE_LOGIN_COMMAND`/`DISABLE_LOGOUT_COMMAND` for Claude Code (`crates/zygo-launch/src/claude_code.rs:129-150`), and `OPENCODE_CONFIG_CONTENT`/`OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR` for OpenCode (`opencode.rs:465`). The Claude Code session markers are `CLAUDECODE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_SESSION`, `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` and `CLAUDE_CODE_BRIDGE_SESSION_ID` (schema `:540`, `:645`, `:1180`, `:637`). The bypass forms are at `help.txt:18`, `:70` and `:149-151`.
14. Critic read 3 evidence. (a) `sweep.rs:20` keeps a session directory whose `results` is a symlink, and `remove_except_results` (`crates/zygo-launch/src/session_dir.rs:86-100`) skips the `results` entry and then fails `remove_dir`, so without AU69 (a) such directories would stay for ever. (b) The proxy has no per-session request state: every session route calls `accept`, which only checks that the session is mapped and active (`crates/zygo-proxy/src/session.rs:593-599`; the status route calls it at `crates/zygo-proxy/src/routes.rs:225`), the heartbeat only sets `last_heartbeat` (`session.rs:602-609`), and the only in-flight count is a global byte semaphore (`crates/zygo-proxy/src/server.rs:110-113`). So AU91 adds two status fields. (c) The launcher restores the previous signal handling when the last `Installed` drops (`crates/zygo-launch/src/signals.rs:50-57`), and `set` gives no handler to a signal that is already ignored (`signals.rs:151-153`), so an ignore set before `Launcher::run` would also reach the agent; AU88 therefore sets none. (d) The early exits that AU92 lists are at the cited `launcher.rs` lines; `session_dir::open` is at `:378-392`.
15. Final pass evidence. (a) `overrides.json` is written only in `crates/zygo/src/cmd/verbs.rs`: `Overrides::save` at `:200` (`crates/zygo-core/src/overrides.rs:32-34`) and the rollback `restore` (`verbs.rs:153`) after a proxy refusal of `redaction content`, which is also sent with `PATCH …/settings` (`verbs.rs:201-202`). (b) The compaction override changes only through `zygo compact` (`crates/zygo/src/cmd/compact.rs:91-92`), the proxy routes it calls (`crates/zygo-proxy/src/routes.rs:209-229`), and the launch-time `--compact` (`launcher.rs:452`). (c) The `compact` MCP tool only returns `compaction requested` (`crates/zygo-mcp/src/tools/compact.rs:22`); the proxy compacts once on that call (§10.2.3 trigger 2) and changes no override. So OP12 needs no proxy-side write. (d) The proxy session exists before `session_dir::open` (`launcher.rs:378`), so a saved `redaction_content` must be sent with `PATCH`. (e) Crate dependencies (`crates/*/Cargo.toml`): `zygo-hooks` → config, core, proxy, results, usage, work; `zygo-launch` → hooks and others; none of core, config, work, proxy depends on hooks or launch, so AU103 has no cycle. (f) The OpenCode plugin has `experimental.chat.system.transform` at `crates/zygo-launch/assets/opencode-plugin.js:53`. (g) `/zygo:<verb>` runs the same verb as the CLI (`crates/zygo/src/cmd/mod.rs:369-371`, §17.1).

## 21. Not resolved

None. The operator decided OP11 (slash commands), OP12 (saved overrides) and OP13 (proxy status fields); the lead resolved the AU64 scope as an author's call.

The `zygo` binary always registers the dispatch, so "no dispatch registered" is not a production case; the known limit names only a prompt hook killed from outside before it prints. A failed `activity.json` change writes its line by class: a persistent condition of the file (a link, not a regular file, not readable or parsable, over 1 MiB) once per turn, a one-off failure (a missed lock, a newer version, a refused over-limit result) on every event where it happens. |

| 2026-11-23 | W§7.4 (Triggers, first bullet; known limit PW6), W§7.5 rule 1, W§14 (new row), W§17 PW6, W§18 #24, #77 | author's call (node E1 rounds 2 and 3; probes on tmux 3.2a, 3.4, 3.7c and 3.8: `fixtures/tmux/e1-hooks-<version>.txt`, `fixtures/tmux/e1-active-pane-<version>.txt`, scripts in `fixtures/tmux/probes/`) | In the tmux 3.2a release source, the target of `window-layout-changed` is the active pane of the window (`notify.c:253-258`, `layout.c:1049`, sent before `window_remove_pane` at `server-fn.c:189-190`). When that pane is the closed one, the hook goes to the current session (`notify.c:69-70`). So for an active pane closed while another session is current, no `window-layout-changed`, `after-kill-pane` or `pane-exited` runs for the workspace; `window-renamed` (after the close of an `sh` pane, also with no name change) and `window-pane-changed` can run (`e1-active-pane-<version>.txt`; `e1-hooks-<version>.txt` cases B, E). The "every case" of the 2026-11-22 entry was wrong for that pane. 3.2a, 3.4 and 3.7c behave the same; 3.8, the release after 3.7c, runs the workspace hook of `pane-exited`, `window-unlinked` and `window-layout-changed` in these cases (cases B, D, E, F). The boundary is 3.8 for the window case and for the active-pane case. The known limit now covers the active-pane case, says "may not be written" (a `window-renamed` can write it sooner) and holds on 3.7c and earlier; #24 kills the pane that is not active; #77 checks each case against its boundary. A `window-pane-changed` hook was rejected (AU90). |
